# Skills · T · part 88

911 entries, triage to triage-inbox-ticket. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **triage**: [3/4 defending-code] Triage a batch of raw security findings. Third step of the find-and-fix loop (/threat-model -&gt; /vuln-scan -&gt; /triage -&gt; /patch); consumes /vuln-scan's VULN-FINDINGS.json, but accepts any scanner output, so it also stands alone on a third-party backlog. Verify each is real, collapse duplicates, re-rank by impact-on-asset x exploitability, and tag with an owner. Takes a directory or file of scanner output and writes TRIAGE.json + TRIAGE.md sorted by what actually needs engineering attention. Use when asked to "triage findings", "validate scanner output", "prioritize vulns",… ([air-gapped/skills](https://github.com/air-gapped/skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/triage) · ★6)
